# Full evaluation results (generated 2026-11-22)

Every official evaluation on disk, one row per output folder, latest timestamped CSV of the folder. Generated by `scripts/evaluation/make_full_results_md.py`; do not edit by hand (rerun it). Interpretation, chosen checkpoints, selection rules and which numbers are pre-fix: [final_evaluation.md](final_evaluation.md).

Folder names: `<prediction>_<rule>[_k<K>|_lam<λ>]_<traffic>_split`. `<prediction>` is the model and epoch (e.g. `rl3_b0.005_ep01`); `__ctrlscorer_` / `__pairscorer_` = scored with another model's heads; `_own_ec[seq]_<base>_n<N>` = EC rule with the own scorer (seq = sequential); `_ec[seq]_` without `own` = control scorer. Abbreviations: NC no at-fault collision, DAC drivable area, DDC driving direction, TLC traffic light, EP ego progress, TTC time to collision, LK lane keeping, HC history comfort, EC two-frame extended comfort; "score 0" = share of valid scenes scored 0; CSV = timestamp of the source file.
